Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the superconductors framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 108. The resulting operator achieves bound O(861/n^2).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the superconductors decomposition. By minimality, the element a^108 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 861-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
